i-ḷ. mēṟ collappaṭṭa iṭaiccoṟkaḷellām taṉittaṉiyē itaṟku itu poruḷeṉa eṭuttu uṇarttappaṭṭaṉavāyiṉum, viṉaiyōṭum, peyarōṭum kuṟikkap pulappaṭṭu maṟṟōru vēṟupāṭṭaṉavāki variṉum, av vēṟupāṭu ārāyntu atuvum ataṟku ilakkaṇamākak koḷka, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. `otta moḻiyāṟ puṇarttaṉa ruṇarttal- tatta marapiṟ ṟōṉṟumaṉ poruḷē' eṉṟavaḻi, maṉ acainilaiyāyiṟṟu.

{&uṟakkun tuṇaiyatō rālamvit tīṇṭi
yiṟappa niḻaṟpayan tāaṅ-kaṟappayaṉun
tāṉciṟi tāyiṉum takkārkaip paṭṭakkāl
vāṉciṟitāp pōrttu viṭum&} (nālaṭi-38) eṉpataṉuḷ, ummai icainiṟai yāyiṟṟu. {&kāṉkeḻu nāṭar paṭarntōṟkuk, kaṇṇum paṭumō eṉṟiciṉ yāṉē&} eṉṟavaḻi, ummai acainilaiyāyiṟṟu. {&tēvarē tiṉṉiṉum vēmpu&} (nālaṭi-112) eṉṟavaḻi, ēkāram ciṟappuk kuṟittu niṉṟatu. maḻaikkuṟi kaṇṭu {maḻai peyyum pōlum} eṉṟavaḻip, pōlum eṉṟatu aiyaṅ kuṟittu niṉṟatu. {&tēvāti tēvaṉavaṉ cēvaṭicērtu maṉṟē&} (cintā-1.) eṉṟavaḻi aṉṟē eṉpatu muḻutum acainilaiyāyiṟṟu. {&ceṉṟī perumaniṟ ṟakaikkunar yārē&} (akam-46) eṉṟavaḻi, ēkāram īṟṟacaiyāyiṟṟu. kuraikaḻal eṉṟavaḻik, kurai eṉpatu olip poruṇmai uṇarttiṟṟu. piṟavum ivvāṟu varuvaṉa vellām ituvē ottākak koḷka.

((ituvum atu))

TC292t

kiḷanta valla aṉṉa piṟavum
kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalāṉ uṇarttaṉar koḷalē.

itu iṭaiccoṟkup puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

i-ḷ. eṭuttōtappaṭṭa iṭaiccolaṉṟi attaṉmaiya piṟavumāki varuvaṉavum icainiṟai yacainilaiyāki varuvaṉavum poruḷpaṭumāṟu aṟintukoḷka, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. avaṟṟuṭ cila varumāṟu: {&kuṉṟuto ṟāṭalum niṉṟataṉ paṇpē&} (murukāṟ-217) eṉṟavaḻik, kuṉṟutoṟu eṉpatu kuṉṟu pala eṉṉum poruṇmai uṇara niṉṟatu. {&ciṟitutavirn tīka māḷaniṉ paricilar&} (mayilai-pa-m-299) eṉṟavaḻi, māḷa eṉpatu acainilai yāyiṟṟu. {&piriyiṉ vāḻā teṉpō teyya' (aiṅkuṟu-) eṉṟavaḻi, teyya eṉpatu acainilai yāyiṟṟu. {&eṉavāṅ koḷḷaḻaṟ paranta tāmarai, veḷḷi nārāṟ pūppeṟ ṟiciṉē&} eṉṟavaḻi, eṉavum, āṅkum acainilaiyāyiṉa. {&añcuva tōru maṟaṉē oruvaṉai, vañcippa tōru mavā&} (kuṟaḷ-366) eṉṟavaḻi, ōrum eṉpaṉa acainilaiyāyiṟṟu. {&arumpeṟa lulaka niṟaiya - viruntu peṟṟaṉarāṟ polikaniṉ pukaḻē&} (puṟam-62) eṉṟavaḻi, āl acainilai yāyiṟṟu. `taṭumāṟu toḻiṟpeyark kiraṇṭu mūṉṟuṅ, kaṭinilai yilavē poruḷvayi ṉāṉa' (vēṟṟumaimayaṅ-10) eṉṟavaḻi, āṉa acainilaiyāyiṟṟu. {&ceyviṉai maruṅkiṟ celavayarn tiyāḻaniṉ-kaipuṉai vallaviṉ ñāṇuḷar tīyē&} (kali-7.) eṉṟavaḻi, yāḻa acainilaiyāyiṟṟu. piṟavum aṉṉa.

iṭaiyiyal muṟṟum.

((8. uriyiyal))

((uriccolliṉ potuvilakkaṇam))

TC293t

uriccoṟ kiḷavi virikkuṅ kālai
icaiyiṉum kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum tōṉṟip
peyariṉum viṉaiyiṉum meytaṭu māṟi
orucoṟ palaporuṭku urimai tōṉṟiṉum
palacol oruporuṭku urimai tōṉṟiṉum
payilā tavaṟṟaip payiṉṟavai cārttit
tattam marapiṟ ceṉṟunilai maruṅkiṉ
eccol lāyiṉum poruḷvēṟu kiḷattal.

ivvōttu eṉṉa peyarttō eṉiṉ, uriccol ōttu eṉṉum peyarttu: uriccol uṇarttiṉamaiyāṟ peṟṟa peyar.

uriccol eṉpatu yātō eṉiṉ, oru vāypāṭṭāṟ collappaṭum poruṭkut tāṉum urittāki varuvatu. ataṉāṉēyaṉṟē,`orucoṟ palaporuṭ kurimai tōṉṟiṉum, palacol oru poruṭkurimai tōṉṟiṉum' eṉa ōtuvārāyiṉar.

eḻuttatikārattuḷ itaṉaik `kuṟaiccoṟ kiḷavi' eṉṟu ōtiṉamaiyāl, vaṭanūlāciriyar tātu eṉṟu kuṟiyiṭṭa coṟkaḷē ivaiyeṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum; avaiyum kuṟaiccollātalāṉ.

aḵtēl, toḻiṟ poruṇmai uṇarttuvaṉa ellām itaṉuḷ ōtiṉārō eṉiṉ, `veḷippaṭu collē kiḷattal vēṇṭā, veḷippaṭa vārā uriccol mēṉa' (cū.2.) eṉṟā rākaliṉ, vaḻakkiṉkaṭ payiṟci illāta coṟkaḷ īṇṭu eṭuttu ōtappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉka.

toḻilāvatu viṉaiyum viṉaikkuṟippu mātaliṉ, avviruvakaic coṟkum aṅkamāki veḷippaṭātaṉa īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉ talaiccūttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, uriccoṟkellām uriyatōr potu ilakkaṇamum, avaṟṟiṟkup poruḷuṇarttun tiṟaṉum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

i-ḷ. uriccoṟ kiḷavi virikkuṅ kālai eṉpatu uriccol ākiya collai virittu uraikkuṅ kālattu, e-ṟu.

icaiyiṉum kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum tōṉṟi eṉpatu collāṉum, kuṟippāṉum, paṇpāṉum pulappaṭṭu, e-ṟu.

collāṟ pulappaṭṭatu - uṟu eṉpatu; itaṉiṉ aḵtuṟum eṉṟavaḻi, mikum eṉṉum poruḷ pulappaṭṭatu.

kuṟippāṟ pulappaṭṭatu - kaṟuttāṉ eṉpatu; oruvaṉmāṭṭuk karumaiyākiya niṟattaik kuṟiyātu, avaṉatu vekuṭciyaik kuṟittaliṟ kuṟippāyiṟṟu.

paṇpāṟ pulappaṭṭatu - vekuṭcikkuk kaṇcivakkum eṉpatu; kaṇṇiṉ civappu atu civattaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya vekuṭciyiṉmēl vantatu.

ivaiyiṟṟai vaṭanūlāciriyar mukkiyam, ilakkaṇai, kauṇam eṉpa.

coṟporuḷpaṭumvaḻic collāṉum, kuṟippāṉum, kuṇattāṉum poruḷpaṭum eṉpatūum, īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟa uriccol im mūvakaiyāṉum poruḷ vēṟupaṭum eṉpatūum kūṟiyavāṟām.

peyariṉum viṉaiyiṉum meytaṭu māṟi eṉpatu uriccollākiya urupu peyariṉkaṇṇum viṉaiyiṉkaṇṇum taṭumāṟi, e-ṟu.

avai taṭumāṟuṅkāl peyarviṉaikaḷaic cārntum, avaṟṟiṟku aṅkamākiyum varum. {uṟuvaḷi} eṉpatu peyaraic cārntu vantatu. {uṟakkoṇṭāṉ} eṉpatu viṉaiyaic cārntu vantatu. {uṟuvaṉ} eṉṟavaḻip peyarkku aṅkamāyiṟṟu. {uṟṟāṉ} eṉṟavaḻi viṉaikku aṅkamāyiṟṟu.

orucoṟ palaporuṭ kurimai tōṉṟiṉum, palacol oruporuṭ kurimai tōṉṟiṉum eṉpatu orucol pala poruṭku urittākit tōṉṟiṉum, pala col oru poruṭku urittākit tōṉṟiṉum, e-ṟu.

orucol oruporuṭ kurittāki varutal collāmaṉ muṭintatām.

payilā tavaṟṟaip payiṉṟavai cārttit-tattam marapiṟ ceṉṟu nilai maruṅkiṉ eṉpatu uriccoṟkaḷ tattam marapiṉāṟ ceṉṟu niṟkumiṭattu, vaḻakkiṉkaṭ payiṉṟu naṭavāta coṟkaḷaip payiṉṟa coṟkaḷōṭu cērtti, e-ṟu.

payilāta collāvaṉa:- uṟu, tava, naṉi; payiṉṟa collāvaṉa mikutal, uṭku, uyarvu eṉpaṉa. cārttutalāvatu iccoṟkaḷ iccolliṉ poruḷpaṭum eṉak kūṭṭutal.

eccol lāyiṉum poruḷvēṟu kiḷattal eṉpatu yātāṉum oru collāyiṉum poruḷvēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuka, e-ṟu.

tōṉṟit taṭumāṟi eṉṉum ceyteṉ eccam urimai tōṉṟiṉum eṉṉum ceyiṉ eccattiṉōṭu oṉṟik kiḷakka eṉṉum viyaṅkōḷōṭu muṭintatu.

uriccoṟ kiḷaviyai viritturaikkuṅ kālattuc, collāṉum kuṟippāṉum paṇpāṉum poruḷ pulappaṭṭup, peyarkkaṇṇum viṉaikkaṇṇum urupu taṭumāṟi, orucoṟ palaporuṭku urimaiyākit tōṉṟiṉum, palacol loruporuṭku urimaiyākit tōṉṟiṉum, tattam marapiṟ ceṉṟu niṟkumiṭattup, payilāta coṟkaḷaip payiṉṟa coṟkaḷōṭu cērtti, yātāṉumoru collāyiṉum poruḷ vēṟupaṭuttu uraikka, aṟivōr eṉṟavāṟu.

((veḷippaṭaiyallā uriccoṟkaḷē ivviyalil kūṟappaṭum eṉṟal))

TC294t

veḷippaṭu collē kiḷattal vēṇṭā
veḷippaṭa vārā uriccol mēṉa.

īṇṭu ōtukiṉṟa uriccol ivai eṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

i-ḷ. vaḻakkiṉkaṇ ellārāṉum aṟiyappaṭṭa col īṇṭu eṭuttu ōtavēṇṭā. aṟiyappaṭāta uriccoṉmēlaṉa, varukiṉṟa cūttiraṅkaḷ, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. veḷippaṭu collāvaṉa uṇṭal eṉpataṟku ayiṟal, micaital eṉavum; uṟaṅkutal eṉpataṟkut tuñcal eṉavum; iṇaiviḻaiccu eṉpataṟkup puṇartal, kalattal, kūṭal eṉavum; accam eṉpataṟku verūutal eṉavum ivvāṟu varuvaṉa. iṉi veḷippaṭa vaḻaṅkātaṉa kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

((uṟu, tava, naṉi, eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC295t

uṟutava naṉiyeṉa varūum mūṉṟum
mikuti ceyyum poruḷa eṉpa.

iḵtu uriccoṟkaḷiṟ cila coṟporuḷ uṇarttumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

ikkaruttu varukiṉṟa cūttiraṅkaṭkum okkum.

i-ḷ. uṟu, tava, naṉi eṉṟu collappaṭṭa mūṉṟu collum mikuti eṉṉum collāṉ aṟiyappaṭum poruḷai uṇarttum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&uṟupuṉal tantula kūṭṭi&} (nālaṭi-185), {&īyātu vīyum uyirtavap palavē&} (puṟam-235), {&vantunaṉi varuntiṉai vāḻiya neñcē&} (akam-19): ivai mikuti uṇarttiyavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.

((uru eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC296t

uruvuṭ kākum.

i-ḷ. uru eṉṉum col uṭku eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&urukeḻu kaṭavuḷ&} uṭku mikka kaṭavuḷ eṉṟavāṟu.

((purai eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC297t

puraiuyar pākum.

i-ḷ. purai eṉpatu uyarvu eṉṉum poruḷpaṭum. e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&puraiya maṉṟa puraiyōr kēṇmai&} (naṟ-1.)

((kuru, keḻu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC298t

kuruvum keḻuvum niṟaṉā kummē.

i-ḷ. kuru eṉpatūum, keḻu eṉpatūum niṟam eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&kurumaṇit tāli&}, {&ceṅkēḻ meṉkoṭi&} (akam-80.); keḻu eṉpatu kēḻ eṉavum varum.

((cellal, iṉṉal eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC299t

cellal iṉṉal iṉṉā maiyē.

i-ḷ. cellal eṉṉum collum, iṉṉal eṉṉum collum iṉṉāmaip poruḷil varum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&maṇaṅkamaḻ viyaṉmār paṇaṅkiya cellal&}, {&veyilpuṟan tarūum iṉṉa liyakkattu&} (malaipaṭu-374.)

((mallal eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC300t

mallal vaḷaṉē.

i-ḷ. mallal eṉṉum col vaḷappam eṉṉum poruḷ tarum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&mallal mālvarai&} (akam-32)

((ē eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC301t

ēpeṟ ṟākum.

i-ḷ. ē eṉṉum col peṟṟutal eṉṉum poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&ēka laṭukkam&} (naṟṟiṇai-116.): peṟṟutal eṉpatu ikkālam paṟṟutal eṉavarum.

((ukappu eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC302t

i-ḷ.{& ukappu eṉṉum col uyartal eṉṉum poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu {&vicumpukan tāṭātu&}

((uvappu eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC303t

e-ṭu {&uvantuvan tārva neñcamoṭu&} (akam-35.)

((payappu eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

e-ṭu. {&alamara lāyam&} (aiṅ-64), {&terumara luḷḷamō ṭaṉṉai tuñcāḷ&}.

((maḻa, kuḻa eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC309t

maḻavum kuḻavum iḷamaip poruḷa.

i-ḷ. maḻa eṉṉum collum, kuḻa eṉṉum collum iḷamai eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&maḻakaḷiṟu&} (puṟam-38), {&kuḻakkaṉṟu&} (nālaṭi-101)

((cīrtti mikupukaḻ))

((mālai eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

e-ṭu. {&kuṟippēval ceyaṉmālaik koḷainaṭai yantaṇīr&} (kali-9.)

((kūrppu, kaḻivu eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC312t

kūrppum kaḻivum uḷḷatu ciṟakkum.

e-ṭu. {&tuṉikū revvamoṭu&} (ciṟupāṇ-39), {&kaḻikaṇṇōṭṭam&} (patiṟ-22.)

((kataḻvu, tuṉaivu eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC313t

kataḻvum tuṉaivum viraiviṉ poruḷē.

i-ḷ. kataḻvu eṉṉum collum, tuṉaivu eṉṉum collum viraivu eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

kataḻ, tuṉai eṉṉum coṟkaḷai iṉitu viḷakkutaṟkup peyarākki ōtiṉār. mēṟ collappaṭṭavaṟṟiṉum, iṉi varuvaṉavaṟṟiṉum kuṟaiccollāki ivvāṟu varuvaṉa aṟintukoḷka.

e-ṭu. {&kataḻpari neṭuntēr&} (naṟṟiṇai-203), {&tuṉaipari turakkum tuñcāc celaviṉ&} (akam-9.)

((atirvu, vitirvu eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC314t

atirvum vitir(ppu)m naṭukkam ceyyum.

e-ṭu. {&etiratāk kākkum aṟiviṉārk killai, atira varuvatōr nōy&} (kuṟaḷ-429), {&vitirppuṟa vaṟiyā vēmak kāppiṉai&} (puṟam-20).

((vārtal, pōkal, oḻukal eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC315t

vārtal pōkal oḻukal mūṉṟum
nērpum neṭumaiyum ceyyum poruḷa.

i-ḷ. vārtal eṉṉum collum, pōkal eṉṉum collum, oḻukal eṉṉum collum nērpu eṉpataṉ poruṇmaiyum, neṭumai eṉpataṉ poruṇmaiyum uṇarttum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu.{&vārntilaṅku vaiyeyiṟṟu&} (kuṟun-14); {&pōkukoṭi maruṅkul&}- ivai nērmai. {&vārkayiṟṟoḻukai&} (akam-173), {&veḷvēl viṭattēroṭu kāruṭai pōki&}, `mālvarai yoḻukiya vāḻai' (ciṟupāṇ-21)- ivai neṭumai.

((tīrtal, tīrttal eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC316t

tīrtalum tīrttalum viṭaṟporuṭ ṭākum.

i-ḷ. tīrtal eṉṉum collum, tīrttal eṉṉum collum viṭutal eṉṉum poruḷ paṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&tuṇaiyiṟ ṟīrnta kaṭuṅkaṇ yāṉai&} (naṟṟiṇai: 108)- itu taṉviṉai. {nōy tīrttāṉ} eṉpatu piṟaviṉai; viṭuttāṉ eṉappaṭum. ivvēṟupāṭṭāṉ iraṇṭāka ōtiṉār.

((keṭavaral, paṇṇai eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC317t

keṭavaral paṇṇai āyiraṇṭum viḷaiyāṭṭu.

i-ḷ. keṭavaral eṉṉum collum, paṇṇai eṉṉum collum viḷaiyāṭṭu eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&keṭavara lāyam&}, {&paṇṇait tōṉṟiya veṇṇāṉku poruḷum&} (meyppāṭṭiyal-1)

((taṭa, kaya, naḷi eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC318t

taṭavum kayavum naḷiyum perumai.

i-ḷ. taṭa eṉṉum collum, kaya eṉṉum collum, naḷi eṉṉum collum perumai eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&taṭakkai&} (puṟam-394), {&kayavāy&} (akam-118), {&naḷimalai&} (puṟam-150.).

TC319t

avaṟṟuḷ,
taṭaveṉ kiḷavi kōṭṭamum ceyyum
kayaveṉ kiḷavi meṉmaiyum ceyyum
naḷiyeṉ kiḷavi ceṟivum ākum.

i-ḷ. mēṟ coṉṉavaṟṟuḷ, taṭa eṉṉum col kōṭutal eṉpataṉ poruṇmaiyum, kaya eṉṉuñ col meṉmai eṉpataṉ poruṇmaiyum, naḷital eṉpatu ceṟital eṉpataṉ poruṇmaiyum paṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&taṭamarupperumai&} (naṟ-120), {&kayan talaikkuḻavi&}, {&naḷiyiruḷ&}.

((ōytal, āytal, niḻattal, cāay eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC326t

ōytal āytal niḻattal cāay
āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam.

i-ḷ. ōytal eṉṉum collum, āytal eṉṉum collum, niḻattal eṉṉum collum, cāay eṉṉum collum ākiya annāṉku collum oru poruṭkuḷḷa aḷaviṉ nuṇukkattaik kāṭṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&vēṉi luḻanta vaṟituyaṅ kōykaḷiṟu&} (kali-7); {&pāyntāynta tāṉaip parintāṉā maintiṉai&} (kali-96); {&niḻatta yāṉai mēypulam paṭara&} (matu-303); {&kayalaṟa letirak kaṭumpuṉaṟ cāay&} (neṭunal-18).

((pulampu eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC327t

pulampē taṉimai.

i-ḷ. pulampu eṉṉum col taṉimai eṉṉum poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&pulippaṟ kōtta pulampumaṇit tāli&} (akam-7)

((tuvaṉṟu eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC328t

tuvaṉṟu niṟaivākum.

i-ḷ. tuvaṉṟu eṉpatu niṟaivu eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&āriyar tuvaṉṟiya pēricai muḷḷūr&} (naṟ-170.)

((murañcal eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC329t

murañcal mutirvē.

i-ḷ. murañcal eṉṉuñ col mutirvu eṉṉum poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&kōṭupala murañciya kōḷi yālam&} (malaipaṭu-268)

((vemmai eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC330t

vemmai vēṇṭal.

i-ḷ. vemmai eṉṉum col vēṇṭal eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&nī veyyōḷōṭu&} (akam-6)

((poṟpu eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC331t

poṟpē polivu.

i-ḷ. poṟpu eṉṉum col polivu eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&peruvarai yaṭukkam poṟpa&} (naṟ-34.)

((vaṟitu eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC332t

vaṟitu ciṟitākum.

i-ḷ. vaṟitu eṉṉum col ciṟitu eṉpataṉ poruḷākum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&vaṟituneṟi yorīi&}

((ēṟṟam eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC333t

ēṟṟam niṉaivum tuṇivum ākum.

i-ḷ. ēṟṟameṉṉum col niṉaivu, tuṇivu eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&kāṉalañ cērppaṉ koṭumai yēṟṟi&} (kuṟun-145)- itu niṉaivu. {&ēṟṟeṉ ṟiraṅkuva ceyyaṟka&}- itu tuṇivu.

((piṇai, pēṇ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC334t

piṇaiyum pēṇum peṭpiṉ poruḷa.

i-ḷ. piṇai eṉṉuñ collum, pēṇ eṉṉum collum peṭpu eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&arumpiṇai yakaṟṟi vēṭṭa ñāṭpiṉum&}; {&amararp pēṇiyu māvuti yaruttiyum&} (puṟam-99.)

((paṇai eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC335t

paṇaiyē piḻaittal peruppu mākum.

i-ḷ. paṇai eṉṉum col piḻaittal eṉpataṉ poruḷum, perumai eṉpataṉ poruḷum paṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&paṇaittuvīḻ pakaḻi&}- itu piḻaippu. {&paṇaittōṉ&} (akam-1)- itu perumai.

((paṭar eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC336t

paṭarē uḷḷal celavum ākum.

i-ḷ. paṭar eṉpatu niṉaittal eṉpataṉ poruḷum, celavu eṉpataṉ poruḷum paṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. `vaḷḷiyōrp paṭarntu puḷḷiṟ pōki' (puṟam-47)- itu niḷaivu. {&kaṟavai kaṉṟuvayiṟ paṭara&} (kuṟun-108)- itu celavu.

((paiyuḷ, ciṟumai eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC337t

paiyuḷuñ ciṟumaiyum nōyiṉ poruḷa.

i-ḷ. paiyuḷ eṉṉum collum, ciṟumai eṉṉum collum nōy eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&paiyuṇ mālai&} (kuṟun-195.); {&ciṟumai yuṟupavō ceypaṟi yalarē&} (naṟ-1)

((eyyāmai eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC338t

eyyā maiyē yaṟiyā maiyē.

i-ḷ. eyyāmai eṉṉum col aṟiyāmai eṉṉum poruḷ paṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&eyyā maiyalai nīyum varuntuti&} (kuṟiñcip-8.)

((naṉṟu eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC339t

naṉṟu peritākum.

i-ḷ. naṉṟu eṉpatu peritu eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&naṉṟu marituṟ ṟaṉaiyāṟ peruma&} (akam-10.)

((tāvu eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC340t

tāvē valiyum varuttamu mākum.

i-ḷ. tāvu eṉṉum col vali eṉpataṉ poruṇmaiyum, varuttam eṉpataṉ poruṇmaiyum paṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&tāvi ṉaṉpoṉ ṟaiiya pāvai&} (akam-212.)- itu vali. {&karuṅkaṭ ṭākkalai perumpiṟi tuṟṟeṉa&} (kuṟun-69.)- itu varuttam.

((tevu eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC341t

tevukkoḷaṟ poruṭṭē.

i-ḷ. tevu eṉṉum col koḷḷutal eṉpataṉ poruḷ paṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&nīrttevvu niraittoḻuvar&} (maturaik-89.)

((tevvu eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC342t

tevvup pakaiyākum.

i-ḷ. tevvu eṉṉum col pakai eṉṉum poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&tevvup pulam&}

((viṟappu, uṟappu, veṟuppu eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC343t

viṟappum uṟappum veṟuppum ceṟivē.

i-ḷ. viṟappu eṉṉum collum, uṟappu eṉṉum collum, veṟuppu eṉṉum collum ceṟivu eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&viṟanta kāppō ṭuṇṇiṉṟu valiyuṟuttum&}; {&uṟanta viñci&}; {&veṟutta kēḷvi viḷaṅkupukaḻk kapilaṉ&} (puṟam-53.)

((viṟappu eṉpataṟku mēlum oru poruḷ))

TC344t

avaṟṟuḷ,
viṟappē verūup poruṭṭum ākum.

i-ḷ. avaṟṟuḷ viṟappu eṉṉum col verūutal eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&avaleṟi yulakkaip pāṭuviṟan tayala&} (perumpāṇ-226.)

((kampalai, cummai, kali, aḻuṅkal eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC345t

kampalai cummai kaliyē yaḻuṅkal
eṉṟivai nāṉkum aravap poruḷa.

i-ḷ. kampalai eṉṉum collum, cummai eṉṉum collum, kali eṉṉum collum, aḻuṅkal eṉṉum collum ivai nāṉku collum aravam eṉṉum poruḷaiyuṭaiya, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&kampalai mūtūr&} (puṟam-54); {&oliperuñ cummaiyoṭu&}; {&kaliko ḷāyam&} (akam-11) ; {&uyavuppuṇarn taṉṟiv vaḻuṅka lūrē&} (naṟ-203).

((aḻuṅkal eṉpataṟku mēlum iruporuḷ))

TC346t

avaṟṟuḷ,
aḻuṅkal irakkamum kēṭum ākum.

i-ḷ. mēṟ collappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ, aḻuṅkal eṉṉum col irakkam eṉpataṉ poruṇmaiyum, kēṭu eṉpataṉ poruṇmaiyum paṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&makavīḻn taḻuṅkiya&}- iḵtu irakkam. {&kuṇaṉaḻuṅkak kuṟṟa muḻainiṉṟu kūṟum&} (nālaṭi-353)- itu kēṭu.

((kaḻum eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC347t

kaḻumeṉ kiḷavi mayakkañ ceyyum.

i-ḷ. kaḻum eṉpatu mayakkam eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&kaḻumiya ñāṭpiṉ&} (kaḷavaḻi - 11)

((ceḻumai eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC348t

ceḻumai vaḷaṉum koḻuppum ākum.

i-ḷ. ceḻumai eṉpatu vaḷaṉ eṉpataṉ poruḷum koḻuppu eṉpataṉ poruḷum paṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&ceḻumpal kuṉṟam&}- itu vaḷam. {&ceḻuntaṭi tiṉṟa cennāy&}- itu koḻuppu.

((viḻumam eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC349t

viḻumam
cīrmaiyum ciṟappum iṭumpaiyum ceyyum.

i-ḷ. viḻumam eṉpatu cīrmai eṉpataṉ poruḷum, ciṟappu eṉpataṉ poruḷum, iṭumpai eṉpataṉ poruḷum paṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&viḻumiyōr-kāṇṭoṟuñ ceyvar ciṟappu&} (nālaṭi-159)- itu cīrmai. {&vēṟṟumai yillā viḻuttiṇaip piṟantu&} (puṟam-27)-itu ciṟappu. {&niṉṉuṟu viḻumaṅ kaḷaintōṉ&} (akam-170)- itu iṭumpai.

((karuvi eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC350t

karuvi tokuti.

i-ḷ. karuvi eṉṉuñ col tokuti eṉṉum poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&karuvi vāṉam&} (puṟam-159).

((kamam eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC351t

kamamniṟain tiyalum.

i-ḷ. kamam eṉṉuñ col niṟaintu eṉpataṉōṭu iyalum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&kamañcūl māmaḻai&} (akam-43) (kuṟun-158) (murukāṟ-7)

((ari eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC352t

ariyē aimmai.

i-ḷ. ari yeṉṉuñ col aitu eṉṉum poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&arimayirt tiraṇmuṉkai&} (puṟam-11).

((kavavu eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC353t

kavavakat tiṭumē.

i-ḷ. kavavu eṉṉuñ col akattiṭutal eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&kaḻūuviḷaṅ kāram kavaiiya mārpē&} (puṟam-19)

((tuvaittal, cilaittal, iraṅkal, iyampal eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC354t

tuvaittalum cilaittalum iraṅkalum iyampalum
icaipporuṭ kiḷavi yeṉmaṉār pulavar.

i-ḷ. tuvaittal eṉṉuñ collum, cilaittal eṉṉuñ collum, iraṅkal eṉṉuñ collum, iyampal eṉṉuñ collum ōcaip poruṇmaiyuṭaiya col, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&varivaḷai tuvaippa&}; {&āmā nallēṟu cilaippa&} (muruku-315); {&ēṟiraṅ kiruḷiṭai&} (kali-46); {&kaṭimaran taṭiyu mōcai taṉṉūr- neṭumatil varaippiṟ kaṭimaṉai yiyampa&} (puṟam-36)

((iraṅkal eṉpataṟku mēlum oru poruḷ))

TC355t

avaṟṟuḷ,
iraṅkal kaḻinta poruṭṭu mākum.

i-ḷ. mēṟ collappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ iraṅkal eṉpatu kaḻintatu eṉṉum poruḷaiyum uṭaittākum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&ceytiraṅ kāviṉai&} (puṟam-10.)

((ilampāṭu, oṟkam eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC356t

ilampā ṭoṟkam āyiraṇṭum vaṟumai.

i-ḷ. ilampāṭu eṉṉuñ collum, oṟkam eṉṉuñ collum vaṟumai yeṉṉuñ colliṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&ilampaṭu pulavar ēṟṟakain niṟaiya&} (malaipaṭu-576); {&okka loṟkañ coliya&} (puṟam-327).

((ñemirtal, pāytal eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC357t

ñemirtalum pāytalum parattaṟ poruḷa.

i-ḷ. ñemirtal eṉṉuñ collum, pāytal eṉṉuñ collum parattal eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&tarumaṇal ñemiriya tirunakar muṟṟattu&} (neṭunal-90); {&pāypuṉal&}

((kavarpu eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC358t

kavarpuvirup pākum.

i-ḷ. kavarpu eṉṉuñ col viruppu eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&kavarnaṭaip puravi&} (akam-130).

((cēr eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC359t

cērē tiraṭci.

i-ḷ. cēr eṉṉuñ col tiraṭci yeṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&cērttuceṟi kuṟaṅku&} (naṟ-170).

((viyal eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC360t

viyaleṉ kiḷavi yakalap poruṭṭē.

i-ḷ. viyal eṉṉuñ col akalam eṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&viyalulakam&}

((pē, nām, urum eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ))

TC361t

pēe nām urumeṉa varūuṅ kiḷavi
āmuṟai mūṉṟum accap poruḷa.

i-ḷ. pēe eṉṉuñ collum, nām eṉṉuñ collum, urum eṉṉuñ collumākiya ammuṟaimaiyaiyuṭaiya mūṉṟu collum accap poruṇmaiyai uṭaiya, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&maṉṟa marāatta pēemutir kaṭavuḷ&} (kuṟun-87); {&nāma nallarāk katuviyāṅku&}; {&urumil cuṟṟamō ṭiruntōṟ kuṟuki&} (perum-447).

((vaya eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC362t

vayavali yākum.

i-ḷ. vayaveṉṉuñ col valiyeṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&vayakkaḷiṟu&} (matu-15)

((vāḷ eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC363t

vāḷoḷi yākum.

i-ḷ. vāḷ eṉṉuñ col oḷiyeṉpataṉ poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&vāṇutal&} (muruku-6).

((tuyavu eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC364t

tuyaveṉ kiḷavi aṟiviṉ ṟiripē.

i-ḷ. tuyavu eṉṉuñ col aṟiviṉ tiripākiya poruḷpaṭum, e-ṟu.

e-ṭu. {&tuyavuṟṟēm yāmāka&}

((uyā eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC365t

uyāvē yuyaṅkal.

e-ṭu. {&paruntirun tuyāviḷi payiṟṟum&} (akam-14)

((ucā eṉpataṉ poruḷ))

TC366t
